- **harness-create**: Agent 工厂入口（创建/优化双模式）。创建：接收自然语言需求，经 8 步 Pipeline（需求澄清→模式匹配→方案审批→构建→验证→可选评测）产出 output/agents/{name}/v1/ 完整 Agent 工程。优化：对已有 Agent 就地增量修改，支持三种输入形态——直接给方向并点名文件 / 提供 session trace 由诊断器分析 / 仅提优化意向由系统评估推荐方向；改动限定点名 ≤2 个文件且不含结构变更，构建前自动快照、越界自动回滚。当用户请求创建/搭建/生成 Agent/Harness，或优化/改进/调整既有 Agent（提到目标 Agent 目录、给出 trace 路径、或要求修改某个 Agent 的行为）时触发。输入为需求文本或"目标 Agent 路径 + 优化意图"，输出为完整工程或就地修订后的 Agent。 ([ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight](https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/HEAD/Co-Sight%20Harness/agent-genesis/skills/harness-create) · ★1.1k)

Requer PRD (seções relevantes) + arquitetura + AGENTS.md como contexto; se a task for de UI, use também `.catalog/interface.md` e mockups/assets em `.catalog/assets/interface/`. ([thaifurforo/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/thaifurforo/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/harness-implementation) · ★5)
- **harness-implementation**: 설계 검토 문서가 이미 존재하는 상태에서 MIL-STD-1553B 하네스 기능을 구현한다. C++ 네이티브 계층과 C# 상위 계층의 책임을 분리하고, 클린 아키텍처, OOP, 한글 XML 주석, TDD 3색 원칙을 지켜 구현할 때 사용한다. ([parksanghoon-sys/TestCode](https://github.com/parksanghoon-sys/TestCode/tree/HEAD/src/MIL-STD-1553B/.agents/skills/harness-implementation))
- **harness-import**: Use when user invokes /harness-import or wants to install plugins from a shared harness.yaml config file. Reads the config, presents each plugin interactively, and generates the Claude Code install commands for the ones the user selects. Handles both Harness Protocol v1 format (version: "1") and the legacy format (version: 1). ([harnessprotocol/harness-kit](https://github.com/harnessprotocol/harness-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/harness-share/skills/harness-import) · ★11)
